+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

+"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from enum import StrEnum
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, TypeVar
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
+
+from autofdtd.version import __version__
+
+Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
+TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
+
+
+class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
+    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
+
+    EQUAL = "equal"
+    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
+
+
+class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
+    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if name is None:
+        return None
+    stripped = name.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
+    return stripped
+
+
+def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
+        value = component.type
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
+        value = component.get("type")
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
+    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
+        return 0
+
+    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
+    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
+        return 100
+    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
+        return 90
+    return 0
+
+
+def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return bool(value.get("name"))
+    return False
+
+
+def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        candidate = value.get("name")
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
+    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
+    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
+    return values
+
+
+class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
+    geometry: object
+    medium: object
+    name: str | None = None
+    priority: int | None = None
+    background_medium: object | None = None
+
+    @field_validator("name")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
+        return _validate_name(value)
+
+    def resolved_priority(
+        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+    ) -> int:
+        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
+        if self.priority is not None:
+            return self.priority
+        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
+
+
+class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
+    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
+    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+
+    @field_validator("structures")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
+
+    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
+
+    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
+        return OrderedDict(
+            (structure.name, structure)
+            for structure in self.structures
+            if structure.name is not None
+        )
+
+    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
+        try:
+            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
+        except KeyError as exc:
+            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
+
+    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
+        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
+        indexed.sort(
+            key=lambda item: (
+                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
+                item[0],
+            )
+        )
+        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
+
+
+class Simulation(Scene):
+    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
+    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    size: Vec3
+    run_time: float
+    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    boundary_spec: object | None = None
+    grid_spec: object | None = None
+    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
+    shutoff: float | None = None
+    courant: float = 0.99
+    subpixel: object | None = None
+    version: str = __version__
+
+    @field_validator("sources")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
+
+    @field_validator("monitors")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
+
+    @field_validator("size")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
+        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
+            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("run_time")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if value <= 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("shutoff")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
+        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("courant")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
+            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
+        return value
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
+        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
+            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
+        for axis in self.symmetry:
+            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
+                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
+        return self

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

new file mode 100644

+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,227 @@
+"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from enum import StrEnum
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, TypeVar
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
+
+from autofdtd.version import __version__
+
+Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
+TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
+
+
+class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
+    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
+
+    EQUAL = "equal"
+    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
+
+
+class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
+    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if name is None:
+        return None
+    stripped = name.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
+    return stripped
+
+
+def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
+        value = component.type
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
+        value = component.get("type")
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
+    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
+        return 0
+
+    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
+    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
+        return 100
+    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
+        return 90
+    return 0
+
+
+def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return bool(value.get("name"))
+    return False
+
+
+def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        candidate = value.get("name")
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
+    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
+    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
+    return values
+
+
+class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
+    geometry: object
+    medium: object
+    name: str | None = None
+    priority: int | None = None
+    background_medium: object | None = None
+
+    @field_validator("name")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
+        return _validate_name(value)
+
+    def resolved_priority(
+        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+    ) -> int:
+        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
+        if self.priority is not None:
+            return self.priority
+        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
+
+
+class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
+    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
+    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+
+    @field_validator("structures")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
+
+    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
+
+    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
+        return OrderedDict(
+            (structure.name, structure)
+            for structure in self.structures
+            if structure.name is not None
+        )
+
+    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
+        try:
+            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
+        except KeyError as exc:
+            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
+
+    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
+        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
+        indexed.sort(
+            key=lambda item: (
+                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
+                item[0],
+            )
+        )
+        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
+
+
+class Simulation(Scene):
+    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
+    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    size: Vec3
+    run_time: float
+    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    boundary_spec: object | None = None
+    grid_spec: object | None = None
+    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
+    shutoff: float | None = None
+    courant: float = 0.99
+    subpixel: object | None = None
+    version: str = __version__
+
+    @field_validator("sources")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
+
+    @field_validator("monitors")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
+
+    @field_validator("size")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
+        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
+            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("run_time")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if value <= 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("shutoff")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
+        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("courant")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
+            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
+        return value
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
+        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
+            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
+        for axis in self.symmetry:
+            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
+                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
+        return self

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

exec

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f7bd50910d1915a8cc02f3288f5510921e61b0cd

+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,227 @@
+"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from enum import StrEnum
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, TypeVar
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
+
+from autofdtd.version import __version__
+
+Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
+TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
+
+
+class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
+    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
+
+    EQUAL = "equal"
+    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
+
+
+class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
+    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if name is None:
+        return None
+    stripped = name.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
+    return stripped
+
+
+def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
+        value = component.type
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
+        value = component.get("type")
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
+    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
+        return 0
+
+    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
+    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
+        return 100
+    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
+        return 90
+    return 0
+
+
+def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return bool(value.get("name"))
+    return False
+
+
+def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        candidate = value.get("name")
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
+    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
+    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
+    return values
+
+
+class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
+    geometry: object
+    medium: object
+    name: str | None = None
+    priority: int | None = None
+    background_medium: object | None = None
+
+    @field_validator("name")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
+        return _validate_name(value)
+
+    def resolved_priority(
+        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+    ) -> int:
+        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
+        if self.priority is not None:
+            return self.priority
+        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
+
+
+class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
+    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
+    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+
+    @field_validator("structures")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
+
+    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
+
+    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
+        return OrderedDict(
+            (structure.name, structure)
+            for structure in self.structures
+            if structure.name is not None
+        )
+
+    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
+        try:
+            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
+        except KeyError as exc:
+            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
+
+    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
+        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
+        indexed.sort(
+            key=lambda item: (
+                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
+                item[0],
+            )
+        )
+        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
+
+
+class Simulation(Scene):
+    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
+    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    size: Vec3
+    run_time: float
+    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    boundary_spec: object | None = None
+    grid_spec: object | None = None
+    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
+    shutoff: float | None = None
+    courant: float = 0.99
+    subpixel: object | None = None
+    version: str = __version__
+
+    @field_validator("sources")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
+
+    @field_validator("monitors")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
+
+    @field_validator("size")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
+        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
+            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("run_time")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if value <= 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("shutoff")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
+        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("courant")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
+            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
+        return value
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
+        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
+            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
+        for axis in self.symmetry:
+            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
+                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
+        return self

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

exec

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f7bd50910d1915a8cc02f3288f5510921e61b0cd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,227 @@
+"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from enum import StrEnum
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, TypeVar
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
+
+from autofdtd.version import __version__
+
+Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
+TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
+
+
+class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
+    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
+
+    EQUAL = "equal"
+    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
+
+
+class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
+    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if name is None:
+        return None
+    stripped = name.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
+    return stripped
+
+
+def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
+        value = component.type
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
+        value = component.get("type")
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
+    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
+        return 0
+
+    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
+    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
+        return 100
+    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
+        return 90
+    return 0
+
+
+def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return bool(value.get("name"))
+    return False
+
+
+def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        candidate = value.get("name")
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
+    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
+    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
+    return values
+
+
+class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
+    geometry: object
+    medium: object
+    name: str | None = None
+    priority: int | None = None
+    background_medium: object | None = None
+
+    @field_validator("name")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
+        return _validate_name(value)
+
+    def resolved_priority(
+        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+    ) -> int:
+        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
+        if self.priority is not None:
+            return self.priority
+        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
+
+
+class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
+    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
+    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+
+    @field_validator("structures")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
+
+    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
+
+    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
+        return OrderedDict(
+            (structure.name, structure)
+            for structure in self.structures
+            if structure.name is not None
+        )
+
+    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
+        try:
+            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
+        except KeyError as exc:
+            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
+
+    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
+        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
+        indexed.sort(
+            key=lambda item: (
+                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
+                item[0],
+            )
+        )
+        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
+
+
+class Simulation(Scene):
+    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
+    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    size: Vec3
+    run_time: float
+    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    boundary_spec: object | None = None
+    grid_spec: object | None = None
+    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
+    shutoff: float | None = None
+    courant: float = 0.99
+    subpixel: object | None = None
+    version: str = __version__
+
+    @field_validator("sources")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
+
+    @field_validator("monitors")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
+
+    @field_validator("size")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
+        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
+            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("run_time")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if value <= 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("shutoff")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
+        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("courant")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
+            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
+        return value
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
+        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
+            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
+        for axis in self.symmetry:
+            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
+                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
+        return self

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

     1	"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	from collections import OrderedDict
     6	from collections.abc import Mapping
     7	from enum import StrEnum
     8	from pathlib import Path
     9	from typing import Any, TypeVar
    10	
    11	from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
    12	
    13	from autofdtd.version import __version__
    14	
    15	Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
    16	TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
    17	
    18	
    19	class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
    20	    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
    21	
    22	    EQUAL = "equal"
    23	    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
    24	
    25	
    26	class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
    27	    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
    28	
    29	    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
    30	
    31	    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    32	        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
    33	        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
    34	
    35	    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
    36	        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
    37	        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
    38	
    39	    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
    40	        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
    41	        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    42	
    43	
    44	def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
    45	    if name is None:
    46	        return None
    47	    stripped = name.strip()
    48	    if not stripped:
    49	        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
    50	    return stripped
    51	
    52	
    53	def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
    54	    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
    55	        value = component.type
    56	        return str(value) if value is not None else None
    57	    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
    58	        value = component.get("type")
    59	        return str(value) if value is not None else None
    60	    return None
    61	
    62	
    63	def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
    64	    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
    65	        return 0
    66	
    67	    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
    68	    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
    69	        return 100
    70	    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
    71	        return 90
    72	    return 0
    73	
    74	
    75	def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
    76	    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
    77	        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
    78	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
    79	        return bool(value.get("name"))
    80	    return False
    81	
    82	
    83	def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
    84	    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
    85	        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
    86	        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
    87	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
    88	        candidate = value.get("name")
    89	        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
    90	    return None
    91	
    92	
    93	def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
    94	    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
    95	    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
    96	        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
    97	    return values
    98	
    99	
   100	class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
   101	    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
   102	
   103	    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
   104	    geometry: object
   105	    medium: object
   106	    name: str | None = None
   107	    priority: int | None = None
   108	    background_medium: object | None = None
   109	
   110	    @field_validator("name")
   111	    @classmethod
   112	    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
   113	        return _validate_name(value)
   114	
   115	    def resolved_priority(
   116	        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
   117	    ) -> int:
   118	        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
   119	        if self.priority is not None:
   120	            return self.priority
   121	        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
   122	
   123	
   124	class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
   125	    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
   126	
   127	    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
   128	    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
   129	    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
   130	    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
   131	
   132	    @field_validator("structures")
   133	    @classmethod
   134	    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
   135	        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
   136	
   137	    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   138	        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
   139	
   140	    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
   141	        return OrderedDict(
   142	            (structure.name, structure)
   143	            for structure in self.structures
   144	            if structure.name is not None
   145	        )
   146	
   147	    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
   148	        try:
   149	            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
   150	        except KeyError as exc:
   151	            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
   152	
   153	    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
   154	        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
   155	        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
   156	        indexed.sort(
   157	            key=lambda item: (
   158	                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
   159	                item[0],
   160	            )
   161	        )
   162	        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
   163	
   164	
   165	class Simulation(Scene):
   166	    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
   167	
   168	    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
   169	    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
   170	    size: Vec3
   171	    run_time: float
   172	    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
   173	    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
   174	    boundary_spec: object | None = None
   175	    grid_spec: object | None = None
   176	    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
   177	    shutoff: float | None = None
   178	    courant: float = 0.99
   179	    subpixel: object | None = None
   180	    version: str = __version__
   181	
   182	    @field_validator("sources")
   183	    @classmethod
   184	    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
   185	        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
   186	
   187	    @field_validator("monitors")
   188	    @classmethod
   189	    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
   190	        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
   191	
   192	    @field_validator("size")
   193	    @classmethod
   194	    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
   195	        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
   196	            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
   197	        return value
   198	
   199	    @field_validator("run_time")
   200	    @classmethod
   201	    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
   202	        if value <= 0.0:
   203	            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
   204	        return value
   205	
   206	    @field_validator("shutoff")
   207	    @classmethod
   208	    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
   209	        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
   210	            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
   211	        return value
   212	
   213	    @field_validator("courant")
   214	    @classmethod
   215	    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
   216	        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
   217	            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
   218	        return value
   219	
   220	    @model_validator(mode="after")
   221	    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
   222	        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
   223	            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
   224	        for axis in self.symmetry:
   225	            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
   226	                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
   227	        return self

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Architecture
     2	
     3	The package scaffold is split so later Phase 1 work can grow without collapsing public
     4	API, compilation, and execution into one layer.
     5	
     6	## Subsystems
     7	
     8	| Module | Responsibility |
     9	| --- | --- |
    10	| `autofdtd.api` | Public object-model entrypoints and compatibility-facing names |
    11	| `autofdtd.core` | Shared tagged-model base classes, serialization helpers, validators |
    12	| `autofdtd.geometry` | Analytic geometry families and wrappers |
    13	| `autofdtd.materials` | Isotropic, dispersive, and anisotropic material models |
    14	| `autofdtd.boundaries` | Boundary containers and boundary-family models |
    15	| `autofdtd.sources` | Source-time profiles and source object models |
    16	| `autofdtd.monitors` | Monitor object models and postprocessing metadata |
    17	| `autofdtd.grid` | Grid planning and subpixel policy surfaces |
    18	| `autofdtd.ir` | Versioned execution IR independent from the public API |
    19	| `autofdtd.compiler` | Scene normalization, lowering, and chunk planning |
    20	| `autofdtd.runtime` | Chunk placement, scheduling, stepping, and execution metrics |
    21	| `autofdtd.kernels` | Warp module conventions and staged kernel families |
    22	| `autofdtd.diagnostics` | Structured logs, progress, validation reports, timing data |
    23	
    24	## Design Constraints
    25	
    26	- Keep scene modeling, scene compilation, runtime stepping, and IO separate.
    27	- Treat Tidy3D compatibility as a tagged typed-object problem, not a loose dict problem.
    28	- Preserve room for chunk-local interior stages, boundary stages, and future overlap-aware scheduling.
    29	- Make unsupported features explicit through validation or feature-matrix policy.
    30	
    31	## Current Backbone
    32	
    33	The first public models live in `autofdtd.api` and `autofdtd.core`:
    34	
    35	- `Structure` carries `geometry`, `medium`, optional `name`, optional explicit `priority`, and
    36	  optional `background_medium`.
    37	- `Scene` carries background `medium`, an ordered tuple of `structures`, and
    38	  `structure_priority_mode`, plus lookup and resolution-order helpers.
    39	- `Simulation` extends `Scene` with domain size and center, runtime controls, sources,
    40	  monitors, boundary and grid references, symmetry, and version tagging.
    41	
    42	These models are frozen, tagged, and JSON-ready so later tasks can lower them into a
    43	separate execution IR without redesigning the public container layer.

 - `autofdtd.diagnostics`: logging, metrics, and validation namespace

+immutable container models for `Simulation`, `Scene`, and `Structure`, with ordered
+structure precedence helpers and JSON-ready serialization for later IR lowering.

diff --git a/docs/architecture.md b/docs/architecture.md
index 54472cba68c706eef2d6af410a98070a2e372483..a8c5c3ef889bbc92b650153fc1d98d14dd5bf6e8

 - Treat Tidy3D compatibility as a tagged typed-object problem, not a loose dict problem.
 - Preserve room for chunk-local interior stages, boundary stages, and future overlap-aware scheduling.
 - Make unsupported features explicit through validation or feature-matrix policy.
+
+## Current Backbone
+
+The first public models live in `autofdtd.api` and `autofdtd.core`:
+
+- `Structure` carries `geometry`, `medium`, optional `name`, optional explicit `priority`, and
+  optional `background_medium`.
+- `Scene` carries background `medium`, an ordered tuple of `structures`, and
+  `structure_priority_mode`, plus lookup and resolution-order helpers.
+- `Simulation` extends `Scene` with domain size and center, runtime controls, sources,
+  monitors, boundary and grid references, symmetry, and version tagging.
+
+These models are frozen, tagged, and JSON-ready so later tasks can lower them into a
+separate execution IR without redesigning the public container layer.

diff --git a/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py b/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f7bd50910d1915a8cc02f3288f5510921e61b0cd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/autofdtd/core/containers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,227 @@
+"""Tagged immutable container models for Phase 1 scene and simulation setup."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from collections import OrderedDict
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from enum import StrEnum
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, TypeVar
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator
+
+from autofdtd.version import __version__
+
+Vec3 = tuple[float, float, float]
+TNamed = TypeVar("TNamed")
+
+
+class StructurePriorityMode(StrEnum):
+    """Policy for resolving implicit structure priorities."""
+
+    EQUAL = "equal"
+    CONDUCTOR = "conductor"
+
+
+class TaggedContainerModel(BaseModel):
+    """Small frozen pydantic base used by the first container models."""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)
+
+    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Return a JSON-ready tagged payload for local serialization and IR handoff."""
+        return self.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True)
+
+    def to_json_text(self, *, indent: int = 2) -> str:
+        """Return a stable JSON string for snapshots or debugging."""
+        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent, exclude_none=True)
+
+    def write_json(self, path: str | Path, *, indent: int = 2) -> None:
+        """Write the tagged payload to disk."""
+        Path(path).write_text(self.to_json_text(indent=indent) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+
+def _validate_name(name: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if name is None:
+        return None
+    stripped = name.strip()
+    if not stripped:
+        raise ValueError("names must not be empty or whitespace-only")
+    return stripped
+
+
+def _component_type_name(component: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(component, BaseModel) and hasattr(component, "type"):
+        value = component.type
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    if isinstance(component, Mapping):
+        value = component.get("type")
+        return str(value) if value is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _default_priority_for_medium(mode: StructurePriorityMode, medium: object) -> int:
+    if mode is not StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR:
+        return 0
+
+    medium_type = _component_type_name(medium)
+    if medium_type == "PECMedium":
+        return 100
+    if medium_type == "LossyMetalMedium":
+        return 90
+    return 0
+
+
+def _has_name(value: object) -> bool:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        return bool(getattr(value, "name", None))
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return bool(value.get("name"))
+    return False
+
+
+def _extract_name(value: object) -> str | None:
+    if isinstance(value, BaseModel):
+        candidate = getattr(value, "name", None)
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        candidate = value.get("name")
+        return str(candidate) if candidate is not None else None
+    return None
+
+
+def _assert_unique_names(values: tuple[TNamed, ...], field_name: str) -> tuple[TNamed, ...]:
+    names = [name for item in values if (name := _extract_name(item))]
+    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
+        raise ValueError(f"{field_name} names must be unique; got {names!r}")
+    return values
+
+
+class Structure(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Geometry plus medium shell with explicit overlap precedence semantics."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Structure")
+    geometry: object
+    medium: object
+    name: str | None = None
+    priority: int | None = None
+    background_medium: object | None = None
+
+    @field_validator("name")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_name_field(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
+        return _validate_name(value)
+
+    def resolved_priority(
+        self, priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+    ) -> int:
+        """Resolve the effective priority under the scene or simulation policy."""
+        if self.priority is not None:
+            return self.priority
+        return _default_priority_for_medium(priority_mode, self.medium)
+
+
+class Scene(TaggedContainerModel):
+    """Ordered structure container with named access helpers."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Scene")
+    medium: object = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    structures: tuple[Structure, ...] = ()
+    structure_priority_mode: StructurePriorityMode = StructurePriorityMode.EQUAL
+
+    @field_validator("structures")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_structure_names(cls, value: tuple[Structure, ...]) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "structures")
+
+    def structure_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(structure.name for structure in self.structures if structure.name is not None)
+
+    def structures_by_name(self) -> OrderedDict[str, Structure]:
+        return OrderedDict(
+            (structure.name, structure)
+            for structure in self.structures
+            if structure.name is not None
+        )
+
+    def get_structure(self, name: str) -> Structure:
+        try:
+            return self.structures_by_name()[name]
+        except KeyError as exc:
+            raise KeyError(f"unknown structure {name!r}") from exc
+
+    def structures_in_resolution_order(self) -> tuple[Structure, ...]:
+        """Return structures in the order they should be applied during materialization."""
+        indexed = list(enumerate(self.structures))
+        indexed.sort(
+            key=lambda item: (
+                item[1].resolved_priority(self.structure_priority_mode),
+                item[0],
+            )
+        )
+        return tuple(structure for _, structure in indexed)
+
+
+class Simulation(Scene):
+    """Top-level Phase 1 simulation shell with tagged container fields."""
+
+    type: str = Field(default="Simulation")
+    center: Vec3 = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
+    size: Vec3
+    run_time: float
+    sources: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    monitors: tuple[object, ...] = ()
+    boundary_spec: object | None = None
+    grid_spec: object | None = None
+    symmetry: tuple[int, int, int] = (0, 0, 0)
+    shutoff: float | None = None
+    courant: float = 0.99
+    subpixel: object | None = None
+    version: str = __version__
+
+    @field_validator("sources")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_source_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "sources")
+
+    @field_validator("monitors")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_monitor_names(cls, value: tuple[object, ...]) -> tuple[object, ...]:
+        return _assert_unique_names(value, "monitors")
+
+    @field_validator("size")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_size(cls, value: Vec3) -> Vec3:
+        if any(component < 0.0 for component in value):
+            raise ValueError("size components must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("run_time")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_run_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if value <= 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("run_time must be positive")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("shutoff")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_shutoff(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
+        if value is not None and value < 0.0:
+            raise ValueError("shutoff must be non-negative")
+        return value
+
+    @field_validator("courant")
+    @classmethod
+    def _validate_courant(cls, value: float) -> float:
+        if not 0.0 < value <= 1.0:
+            raise ValueError("courant must be in the interval (0, 1]")
+        return value
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _validate_symmetry(self) -> Simulation:
+        if len(self.symmetry) != 3:
+            raise ValueError("symmetry must contain exactly three axes")
+        for axis in self.symmetry:
+            if axis not in (-1, 0, 1):
+                raise ValueError("symmetry values must be -1, 0, or 1")
+        return self
diff --git a/tests/test_containers.py b/tests/test_containers.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1c2ab4caf32267710a881c73c6a8d0d0fd11c64d

+++ b/tests/test_containers.py
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import BaseModel
+
+from autofdtd.api import Scene, Simulation, Structure, StructurePriorityMode
+
+
+class NamedStub(BaseModel):
+    type: str
+    name: str | None = None
+
+
+def test_structure_name_is_trimmed() -> None:
+    structure = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="  core  ",
+    )
+
+    assert structure.name == "core"
+
+
+def test_scene_rejects_duplicate_structure_names() -> None:
+    structure_a = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+    structure_b = Structure(geometry={"type": "Sphere"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="wg")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="structures names must be unique"):
+        Scene(structures=(structure_a, structure_b))
+
+
+def test_scene_resolution_order_uses_priority_then_insertion_order() -> None:
+    low = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="low", priority=0)
+    high = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Sphere"},
+        medium={"type": "Medium"},
+        name="high",
+        priority=10,
+    )
+    equal_late = Structure(geometry={"type": "Cylinder"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="late")
+
+    scene = Scene(structures=(high, low, equal_late))
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == ["low", "late", "high"]
+
+
+def test_scene_conductor_mode_assigns_default_medium_priorities() -> None:
+    dielectric = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="dielectric")
+    lossy = Structure(
+        geometry={"type": "Box"},
+        medium={"type": "LossyMetalMedium"},
+        name="lossy",
+    )
+    pec = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "PECMedium"}, name="pec")
+
+    scene = Scene(
+        structures=(pec, dielectric, lossy),
+        structure_priority_mode=StructurePriorityMode.CONDUCTOR,
+    )
+
+    assert [item.name for item in scene.structures_in_resolution_order()] == [
+        "dielectric",
+        "lossy",
+        "pec",
+    ]
+
+
+def test_simulation_requires_unique_source_and_monitor_names() -> None:
+    repeated = NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="port")
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sources names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, sources=(repeated, repeated))
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="monitors names must be unique"):
+        Simulation(size=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), run_time=1.0, monitors=(repeated, repeated))
+
+
+def test_simulation_serialization_payload_is_tagged_and_json_ready(tmp_path) -> None:
+    simulation = Simulation(
+        center=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
+        size=(10.0, 6.0, 2.0),
+        run_time=50.0,
+        medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 1.0},
+        structures=(
+            Structure(
+                geometry={"type": "Box", "center": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], "size": [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]},
+                medium={"type": "Medium", "permittivity": 3.4},
+                name="core",
+            ),
+        ),
+        sources=(NamedStub(type="PointDipole", name="src"),),
+        monitors=(NamedStub(type="FieldMonitor", name="fields"),),
+        boundary_spec={"type": "BoundarySpec"},
+        grid_spec={"type": "GridSpec"},
+        subpixel={"type": "SubpixelSpec"},
+    )
+
+    payload = simulation.to_payload()
+    assert payload["type"] == "Simulation"
+    assert payload["structures"][0]["type"] == "Structure"
+    assert payload["sources"][0]["type"] == "PointDipole"
+
+    output_path = tmp_path / "simulation.json"
+    simulation.write_json(output_path)
+    restored = json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    assert restored["monitors"][0]["name"] == "fields"
+
+
+def test_scene_named_lookup_preserves_insertion_order() -> None:
+    first = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="first")
+    second = Structure(geometry={"type": "Box"}, medium={"type": "Medium"}, name="second")
+    scene = Scene(structures=(first, second))
+
+    assert list(scene.structures_by_name()) == ["first", "second"]
+    assert scene.get_structure("second") is second

apply patch
